Implement:
def project_box_sum(v, target):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=float)
    # first handle trivial: compute clipped sum at t=0?
    # find t via binary
    # If sum(v) close target and v within [0,1], just return v.
    if np.all((v >= -1e-12) & (v <= 1+1e-12)) and abs(v.sum()-target)<1e-8:
         h=np.clip(v,0,1); adjust exact; return h
    lo = float(np.min(v)) - 1.0
    hi = float(np.max(v)) + 1.0
    # If at lo, sum= n > target usually; at hi, sum=0. target between 0,n.
    for _ in range(50):
       mid=(lo+hi)/2
       s=(np.clip(v-mid,0,1)).sum()
       if s > target: # need increase t to reduce sum
           lo=mid
       else:
           hi=mid
    t=(lo+hi)/2
    h=np.clip(v-t,0,1)
    # exact adjust
    # Note due t, sum within 1e-10. correct residual
    res = target - h.sum()
    if abs(res) > 0:
       # adjust elements not at bounds, else all
       free = (h > 1e-12) & (h < 1-1e-12)
       if free.any():
          h[free] += res / free.sum() maybe if small. But to be exact, set one idx:
          idx = np.where(free)[0][0]; h[idx] += res
       else:
          # distribute to all
          h += res / len(h)
       h = np.clip(h,0,1)
       # Maybe sum drifted after clip; do a final small correction
       res2 = target - h.sum()
       if abs(res2) > 1e-15:
          # choose idx with room in direction
          if res2>0:
             idx = np.argmax(1-h)
          else:
             idx = np.argmin(h)
          h[idx] += res2
          h = np.clip(h,0,1)
    return h

def fast_corr_raw(h, one_minus_h, n, N):
   fa = np.fft.rfft(h, N)
   fb = np.fft.rfft(one_minus_h, N)
   r = np.fft.irfft(fa * np.conj(fb), N)
   # mapping to full
   if n == 1: return r[0]? not relevant
   return np.concatenate((r[-(n-1):], r[0:n-1]))

def fgrad_smooth(h,n,alpha,N):
  b=1-h
  fa=np.fft.rfft(h,N); fb=np.fft.rfft(b,N)
  r=np.fft.irfft(fa*np.conj(fb),N)
  raw = concat... ; c=raw*dx; c=np.maximum(c,0)
  m=c.max()
  z=alpha*(c-m); ez=np.exp(z); S=ez.sum(); w=ez/S * dx; f=m + log(S)/alpha
  # gradient
  W=w
  w0=W[n-1]
  a=np.zeros(n); b_arr=np.zeros(n)
  if n>1:
    a[1:] = W[n-2:0:-1]? Wait indices: W index n-1-d for minus d. d=1..n-1. a[d]=W[n-1-d]. For d ascending, a[1:] = W[n-2:0:-1] (since d=1 -> n-2, d=n-1 -> 0). Good.
    b_arr[1:] = W[n:2*n-2+1]? d=1 -> n, ... d=n-1 -> 2n-2. W[n:2*n-1]. Slice W[n:2*n-1] length n-1. Good.
  s=a+b
  # tail a prefix b
  # Use vector cumsum
  if n>1:
     a_pos=a[1:]
     # tail: suffix sums of a_pos
     tail_prefix = np.concatenate((np.cumsum(a_pos[::-1])[::-1], [0.0]))
     b_pos=b_arr[1:]
     pref = np.concatenate(([0.0], np.cumsum(b_pos)))
  else tail=pref=[0]
  # F and H via convolution using fa and s? Need fs = rfft(s,N). H = irfft(fa*fs,N)[:n]
  # F = irfft(fa * conj(fs), N)[:n]
  fs=np.fft.rfft(s,N)
  F=np.fft.irfft(fa * np.conj(fs), N)[:n]
  H=np.fft.irfft(fa * fs, N)[:n]
  G = w0*(1-2*h) + tail - F + pref - H
  # Multiply? W already dx-scale; no extra.
  # But note c weights W=dx*normalized; w0 = dx*norm_lag0. Formula gives ∇ (dx * raw) = dx * ∇raw? Yes.
  # However raw corr R may have convolution; gradient formula uses linear truncated pairs. It doesn't need N. Should be exact.
  # Due FFT small imag, real.
  G = np.real(G)
  return f,c,W_norm? G

- Input h, alpha, time_limit, max_iter.

   Try Armijo:
     accept=False

       h_try = project(h - s*g)
       f_try, c_try = eval (or compute ftry)
       if f_try <= f - 1e-4*s*np.dot(g,g): accept; break
       step = 0.5*s
     if not accept:
       # maybe step too small; use randomized nudge or break
       h_try = project(h - 0.01*step? or best)
   Update momentum:
     if accepted:
       v = beta*v - s*g  (v step direction)
       h = h_try
       step = max(0.1, min(2, step * 1.5?)) maybe
       # Use BB: if last grad, step = dot(dx, dg)/dot(dg,dg)
   else break.

def fast_len(n):
    m=1
    need=2*n-1
    while m < need: m <<=1
    return m

fast_corr_length:
def raw_corr(h, n, N):
   b = 1-h
   fa = np.fft.rfft(h, N)
   fb = np.fft.rfft(b, N)
   r = np.fft.irfft(fa * np.conj(fb), N)
   out = np.empty(2*n-1)
   out[:n-1] = r[-(n-1):]
   out[n-1:] = r[:n]
   return out
This uses r[-(n-1):] where n-1 positive. For n=1? n at least maybe 50. okay. To avoid copying? fine.
Could reuse fa for gradient; define `corr_and_grad` to compute fa once. But for simplicity in fgrad we can compute fa, fb, raw. There will be additional rfft for s. We can make fgrad compute all.

def logsum_values(c, alpha):
   m=c.max()
   z=alpha*(c-m)
   ez=np.exp(z)
   S=ez.sum()
   f=m + np.log(S)/alpha
   w=ez/S
   return f,w

For target penalty:
def penalty_values(c,T):
   v=np.maximum(c-T,0)
   phi=(v*v).sum()
   gweights=v.copy()  # or 2v not needed for direction
   return phi,gweights

Direct weighted gradient:
def grad_direct(h,W,n):
    G=np.zeros(n)
    G += W[n-1]*(1-2h)
    for d in range(1,n):
       wm=W[n-1-d]; wp=W[n-1+d]
       if abs(wm)+abs(wp) < 1e-15: continue
       S=wm+wp
       # left contributions for i=0..n-d-1: wm - S h[i+d]
       if wm!=0 or S!=0:
          G[:n-d] += wm - S*h[d:]
       # right contributions for i=d..n-1: wp - S h[:n-d]
       if wp!=0 or S!=0:
          G[d:] += wp - S*h[:n-d]
    return G
Check: For distance d, left i=0..n-d-1 pair (i,i+d): G_i += wm - S h_{i+d} (wm=w_-). Right i=d..n-1 pair (i-d,i): G_i += wp - S h_{i-d}. Good. This is O(K n) but K active small. For K up to 50 maybe 50*400*2=40k vector ops, okay. For K=800 all, vector slices many Python loops 800, maybe slower but still maybe okay? 800 loops per gradient ~ maybe 0.5ms? Actually vector slice operations each alloc? Could be ~0.1ms*800=80ms, too high. Use FFT for dense weights.

Using FFT weighted_grad:
def grad_fft(h,W,n,N,fa=None):
    if fa is None: fa=np.fft.rfft(h,N)
    w0=W[n-1]
    a=np.zeros(n); b=np.zeros(n)
    if n>1:
      a[1:]=W[n-2::-1]  # W[n-2],...,W[0]
      b[1:]=W[n:2*n-1]
    s=a+b
    # if s all zero and w0 zero? return
    # tail/pref
    if n>1:
      ap=a[1:]; bp=b[1:]
      tail = np.concatenate((np.cumsum(ap[::-1])[::-1], np.array([0.0])))
      pref = np.concatenate((np.array([0.0]), np.cumsum(bp)))
    else: tail=np.array([0.0]); pref=np.array([0.0])
    fs=np.fft.rfft(s,N)
    F=np.fft.irfft(fa * np.conj(fs), N)[:n]
    H=np.fft.irfft(fa * fs, N)[:n]
    G = w0*(1-2*h) + tail - F + pref - H
    return np.real(G)
But if W dense and fa from h, need raw corr? fgrad can pass fa to avoid recompute. But grad_fft also uses `s` rfft and 2 irfft. Good.

Projected gradient algorithm with Armijo:
def projected_pg(h,n,N,alpha,deadline,max_iter,lr0=0.05,momentum=0.0,active_target=None):
   best_h,best_c = h, max_c(h)
   v = zeros
   prev_h=None; prev_g=None; step=lr0
   accept_streak=0; stall=0
   for it in range(max_iter):
      if time > deadline break
      f,g = fgrad_smooth(h,n,alpha,N)
      cmax = f? Wait f not cmax. fgrad returns cmax too.
      gnorm = norm(g)
      if gnorm < 1e-9: break
      # BB step
      if prev_g is not None:
         dxv = h - prev_h
         dgv = g - prev_g
         denom = dot(dgv,dgv)
         if denom > 1e-12:
             num = dot(dxv,dgv)
             # standard BB
             if num > 0:
                step_bb = num / denom
                step = 0.5*step + 0.5*min(max(step_bb, 1e-4), 0.2/gnorm?) maybe
      # Line search: try steps = step, step*0.5, step*0.25, ...
      base_dir = g + momentum*(g - prev_g) if momentum? But for Armijo, use direction d.
      We can use heavy ball: d = g + momentum*(g-prev_g) (if momentum>0). But line search with d.
      norm_d = norm(d)
      c1=1e-4
      accept=False
      s=step if norm_d>0 else 1
      for _ in range(30):
         h_try = project(h - s*d)
         f_try = eval_f(h_try)
         if f_try <= f - c1*s*dot(d,d):
             accept=True; break
         s *=0.5
      if not accept:
         # try a tiny step or restart
         s = max(1e-6, step*0.01)
         h_try = project(h - s*d)
         f_try=eval_f(...)
         if f_try < f: accept=True
         else:
            # random restart from best with small noise
            h = best_h + 0.02*randn, project; v reset; step=lr0; stall=0; continue
      if accept:
         # update step for next
         step = max(step*1.5, 0.01) if f_try < f - 0.1*? else step*0.7
         prev_h, prev_g = h, g
         h = h_try; prev_h? For BB next uses h and g? We need g at h_try. We can set prev_h = old_h, prev_g=old_g after update? Usually BB uses previous and current iterates after line search: dx = x_new - x_old, dg = g_new - g_old. So need compute g_new. We currently have old g; after h_try, next loop computes g_new. Store old_h, old_g. At next iteration, prev_h=old? Let's manage:
         old_h = h; old_g = g
         h = h_try
         # set prev_h, prev_g = old? Next iteration current h and g_new; BB uses h - prev_h (current - old) and g_new - prev_g (new - old). So prev should be old. Yes.

         # momentum maybe reset? If using d with momentum, not.
         if cmax < best_c: best_c=cmax; best_h=h; stall=0
         else stall+=1
      if stall > 1000: restart perturb
   return best_h,best_c

```
import numpy as np
import scipy.optimize as opt
from scipy.optimize import minimize
import time

def nfft_len(n):
    need=2*n-1
    m=1
    while m<need: m <<=1
    return m

def project_box_sum(v, target):
    v=np.asarray(v, dtype=float)
    # quick if feasible
    # binary search shift
    lo = float(np.min(v)) - 1.0
    hi = float(np.max(v)) + 1.0
    # ensure target within [0,n]
    # 60 iterations
    # Use np.clip and sum
    for _ in range(60):
       mid=(lo+hi)*0.5
       s=float(np.clip(v-mid,0.0,1.0).sum())
       if s > target:
          lo=mid
       else:
          hi=mid
    t=(lo+hi)*0.5
    h=np.clip(v-t,0.0,1.0)
    # exact correction
    for _ in range(2):
       res=float(target - h.sum())
       if abs(res) <= 1e-13: break
       if res > 0:
          idx=int(np.argmax(1.0-h))
       else:
          idx=int(np.argmin(h))
       # adjust only within bounds
       if res > 0 and h[iidx] >=1: ...
       h[idx] += res
       h=np.clip(h,0,1)
       res=float(target-h.sum())
       if abs(res)>1e-14:
          # distribute among free
          free=(h>1e-12)&(h<1-1e-12)
          if free.any(): h[free]+=res/free.sum(); h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    return h
```

Fast corr:
```
def fft_corr_raw(h,b,n,N):
    fa=np.fft.rfft(h,N); fb=np.fft.rfft(b,N)
    r=np.fft.irfft(fa*np.conj(fb),N)
    out=np.empty(2*n-1,float)
    out[:n-1]=r[-(n-1):]
    out[n-1:]=r[:n]
    return out
```

```
def c_values(h,n,N):
    raw = fft_corr_raw(h,1-h,n,N)
    c=raw*(2.0/n)
    # FFT numerical negative
    np.maximum(c,0.0,out=c)
```

Logsum and weights:
```
def softmax_weights(c, alpha):
    m=float(c.max())
    z=alpha*(c-m)
    ez=np.exp(z)
    S=float(ez.sum())
    if S <=0: ez=np.zeros_like(c); ez[np.argmax(c)]=1; S=1
    w=ez/S
    f=m + np.log(S)/alpha
    return f,m,w
```

Gradient with weights:
```
def weighted_grad(h,W,n,N,fa=None):
    if W.size != 2*n-1: ...
    # count active d
    if n>1:
       dz = np.abs(W[n-2::-1]) + np.abs(W[n:2*n-1])
       active = int(np.count_nonzero(dz > 1e-14))
    else: active=0
    if active <= 48:
       G = np.zeros(n)
       W0=W[n-1]
       if W0: G += W0*(1-2*h)
       for d in range(1,n):
          wm = W[n-1-d]; wp = W[n-1+d]
          if abs(wm) <=1e-14 and abs(wp) <=1e-14: continue
          S=wm+wp
          if S or wm: G[:n-d] += wm - S*h[d:]
          if S or wp: G[d:] += wp - S*h[:n-d]
       return G
    # FFT
    if fa is None: fa=np.fft.rfft(h,N)
    a=np.zeros(n); bnp=np.zeros(n)
    a[1:]=W[n-2::-1]
    bnp[1:]=W[n:2*n-1]
    s=a+bnp
    if n>1:
       ap=a[1:]
       tail=np.concatenate((np.cumsum(ap[::-1])[::-1], np.array([0.0])))
       bp=bnp[1:]
       pref=np.concatenate((np.array([0.0]), np.cumsum(bp)))
    else:
       tail=np.array([0.0]); pref=np.array([0.0])
    fs=np.fft.rfft(s,N)
    F=np.fft.irfft(fa*np.conj(fs), N)[:n]
    H=np.fft.irfft(fa*fs, N)[:n]
    G = W[n-1]*(1.0-2.0*h) + tail - F + pref - H
    return np.real(G)
```
But in direct, if `S` variable name conflicts with sum; okay.

```
def smooth_fun_lbg(x,n,alpha,N,pen):
    dx=2.0/n
    b=1.0-x
    fa=np.fft.rfft(x,N); fb=np.fft.rfft(b,N)

    m,float? m=c.max()
```

def weighted_fun(x, n, N, Wnorm, pen):
   # Wnorm sum 1 over lags for c
   fa=np.fft.rfft(x,N); fb...
   raw...
   c=raw*dx; np.maximum(c,0,out=c)
   f=float(np.dot(c, Wnorm))
   Wfull=dx*Wnorm
   g=weighted_grad(x, Wfull, n, N, fa)
   if pen: s=x.sum()-n/2; f += .5*pen*s*s; g += pen*s
   return f,g
```

Active set selection:
```
def choose_active(c, K, tol):
    thresh = c.max() - tol
    idx = np.where(c >= thresh)[0]
    if len(idx) > K:
       # take top K by c
       top = np.argsort(c)[-K:][::-1]
       # maybe include all above max - min_gap? Could just K
       return top
    return idx if len(idx)>0 else [int(np.argmax(c))]
```
Use K=8,16,32. Tolerance maybe 1e-5, 1e-4. At high precision, tol 1e-5.

Pseudo run:
```
def run(seed,budget_s):
 t0=time.time(); total=budget_s-5; deadline=t0+total
 try h0=...
 n0=len(h0); N0=nfft_len(n0)
 best_h=project(h0,n0/2)
 best_c=exact_c(best_h,n0)
 best_n=n0
 rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
 # Phase A: logsum L-BFGS at n0
 for alpha in [100,500,2000,10000,50000,100000,500000]:
    if time near deadline break
    h=lbfgs_smooth(best_h,n0,alpha,N0,pen=1e5,maxiter=300,deadline=... maybe overall minus reserve)
    h=project
    c=exact; if c<best: update
 # Phase B: active-set iterations at best_n
 active Ks = [(8,1e-4),(16,1e-3),(32,2e-3),(16,1e-4),(8,1e-5),(32,1e-5),(64,2e-5)]
 for K,tol in active_Ks:
    if time remaining < 10 break
    h=best_h
    for rep in range(3):
       c0, cvals = compute_c_fast(h,best_n,N_best)
       idx = choose_active(cvals,K,tol)
       Wnorm=zeros; Wnorm[idx]=1/len(idx)
       h=lbfgs_weighted(h,best_n,N_best,Wnorm,pen=1e5,maxiter=400,deadline=...)
       h=project; c=exact; if c<best: update
       # maybe if no improvement and new top unchanged break
 # Phase C: multi-start perturb from best at best_n
 scales = [0.001,0.002,0.005,0.01,0.02,0.04,0.004,0.008,0.015,0.03,0.05]
 for scale in scales while time:
    h=best_h + scale*rng.randn(n_best); project
    # short active/logsum: e.g. active K=8 tol=1e-4 maxiter 300; logsum alpha=50000 maxiter200
    if exact improves update
 # Phase D: higher resolution
 for target_n in [600,800,1000?] while time > 60:
    if target_n <= best_n and no reason? maybe try after best_n=400.
    h_up=upsample(best_h,best_n,target_n); project
    # quick active/logsum optimize at n=target_n
    # use same active and logsum fewer
    if exact improves update best_n=target_n,h
 # final: if time, final logsum high alpha at best_n then exact
 return best_h,best_c,best_n
```

Example:
```
def job_deadline(frac=0.25, min_s=5, hard=...):
    rem = hard - time.time()
    if rem < min_s: return time.time()
    return min(hard-min_s, time.time()+max(min_s, frac*rem))

_TIMEOUT_DT = 0.0
_LAST_XK = None

class OptTimeout(Exception):
    pass

def time_callback(xk):
    if time.time() > _TIMEOUT_DT:
        global _LAST_XK
        _LAST_XK = xk.copy()
        raise OptTimeout()
```

`lbfgs_opt(fun, x0, args, deadline, maxiter, pen=None?)`:
```
def lbfgs_opt(fun, x0, args, deadline, maxiter, constant_step=None):
   global _TIMEOUT_DT, _LAST_XK
   if deadline - time.time() < 1: return x0
   _TIMEOUT_DT = deadline - 0.2
   _LAST_XK = None
   options = {'maxiter': maxiter, 'ftol': 1e-15, 'gtol': 1e-8, 'maxls': 40}
   try:
      res = minimize(fun, x0, args=args, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, bounds=Bounds(0.0,1.0), options=options, callback=time_callback)
      xnew = res.x
   except OptTimeout:
      xnew = _LAST_XK if _LAST_XK is not None else x0
   except Exception:
      xnew = x0
   return xnew
```

Function top-level:
```
def localized_weights(n, c, K, sigma):
   full_len=2*n-1
   if K > full_len: K=full_len
   idx=np.argpartition(c, full_len-K)[full_len-K:] # top K, if K small. But argpartition O(n). fine.
   vals=c[idx]
   m=vals.max()
   w=np.exp((vals-m)/sigma)
   s=w.sum()
   W=np.zeros(full_len)
   W[idx]=w/s
   return W
```
If sigma very small and top values close, okay. If K=1, weight leader.
Use sigma >=1e-6. For active set dynamic, use `c` from fast c.
```

from scipy.optimize import minimize, Bounds
import time

class OptTimeout(Exception):
    pass

_TIMEOUT_DT = 0.0
_LAST_XK = None

def time_callback(xk):
    global _LAST_XK
    if time.time() > _TIMEOUT_DT:
        _LAST_XK = np.array(xk, dtype=float).copy()
        raise OptTimeout()

def fft_len_for(n):

def finish_feasible(h,target):
    for _ in range(3):
       res=target-h.sum()
       if abs(res)<1e-13: break
       if res>0: idx=int(np.argmax(1-h))
       else: idx=int(np.argmin(h))
       h[idx]+=res
       h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    return h

def fast_c_full(h,n,N):
    fa=np.fft.rfft(h,N); fb=np.fft.rfft(1.0-h,N)
    r=np.fft.irfft(fa*np.conj(fb),N)
    raw=np.empty(2*n-1,float)
    raw[:n-1]=r[-(n-1):]
    raw[n-1:]=r[:n]
    c=raw*(2.0/n)
    np.maximum(c,0.0,out=c)

```

`weighted_grad` code. Need be careful with slicing for n=1? n even >1. But implement n=1 robust.
```
def weighted_grad(h,W,n,N,fa=None):
    G=np.zeros(n,float)
    if W is None or n==0: return G
    w0=W[n-1]
    if w0: G += w0*(1.0-2.0*h)
    if n == 1: return G
    a=np.zeros(n); b=np.zeros(n)
    a[1:]=W[n-2::-1]
    b[1:]=W[n:2*n-1]
    # active direct threshold
    dz=np.abs(a[1:])+np.abs(b[1:])
    active=int(np.count_nonzero(dz > 1e-14))
    if active <= 48:
       for d in range(1,n):
          wm=a[d]; wp=b[d]
          if abs(wm) <= 1e-14 and abs(wp) <=1e-14: continue
          S=wm+wp
          if wm != 0.0 or S != 0.0:
             G[:n-d] += wm - S*h[d:]
          if wp != 0.0 or S != 0.0:
             G[d:] += wp - S*h[:n-d]
       return G
    if fa is None: fa=np.fft.rfft(h,N)
    s=a+b
    ap=a[1:]; bp=b[1:]
    tail=np.concatenate((np.cumsum(ap[::-1])[::-1], np.array([0.0])))
    pref=np.concatenate((np.array([0.0]), np.cumsum(bp)))
    fs=np.fft.rfft(s,N)
    F=np.fft.irfft(fa*np.conj(fs),N)[:n]
    H=np.fft.irfft(fa*fs,N)[:n]
    G = w0*(1.0-2.0*h) + tail - F + pref - H
    return np.real_if_close(G, tol=1000).real? 
```
`np.real_if_close` maybe returns complex if not; use `G = np.real(G)`.
For direct, if W is normalized c weights (sum dx) and n small, `w0` etc. Good.
```

if len(idxs) <=48:

```
`weighted_fun` for L-BFGS:
```
def weighted_fun(x,n,N,Wnorm,pen):
    dx=2.0/n
    fa=np.fft.rfft(x,N)
    fb=np.fft.rfft(1.0-x,N)
    r=np.fft.irfft(fa*np.conj(fb),N)
    raw=np.empty(2*n-1,float)
    raw[:n-1]=r[-(n-1):]
    raw[n-1:]=r[:n]
    c=raw*dx
    np.maximum(c,0.0,out=c)
    f=float(np.dot(c,Wnorm))
    Wfull=dx*Wnorm
    g=weighted_grad(x,Wfull,n,N,fa)
    if pen != 0:
       s=x.sum() - n/2.0
       f += 0.5*pen*s*s
       g = g + pen*s
    return f,g
```
If Wnorm is None? no.
`np.dot(c,Wnorm)` if Wnorm sum1. If Wnorm float. Good.

`smooth_fun_lbg`:
```
def smooth_fun_lbg(x,n,alpha,N,pen):
    dx=2.0/n
    fa=np.fft.rfft(x,N)
    fb=np.fft.rfft(1.0-x,N)
    r=...
    raw...
    c=raw*dx; clip
    m=float(c.max())
    z=alpha*(c-m)
    ez=np.exp(z)
    S=float(ez.sum())
    if S <= 0:
       w=np.zeros_like(c); w[int(np.argmax(c))]=1.0; S=1.0
    else:
       w=ez/S
    f=m+np.log(S)/alpha
    g=weighted_grad(x,dx*w,n,N,fa)
    if pen: ...
    return f,g
```
If alpha=0? not.

`lbfgs_predict` generic:
```
def lbfgs_run(fun,x0,args,maxiter,deadline,pen? no pen in args):
   if time.time() >= deadline: return x0
   if not np.all(np.isfinite(x0)): return x0
   global _TIMEOUT_DT, _LAST_XK
   _TIMEOUT_DT = deadline - 0.3
   _LAST_XK = None
   try:
       opts={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-14,'gtol':1e-8,'maxls':40}
       res=minimize(fun,x0,args=args,method='L-BFGS-B',jac=True,bounds=Bounds(0.0,1.0),options=opts,callback=time_callback)
       xnew=res.x
   except OptTimeout:
       xnew=_LAST_XK if _LAST_XK is not None else x0
   except Exception:
       xnew=x0
   if xnew is None or not np.all(np.isfinite(xnew)):
       xnew=x0
   return xnew
```
But `bounds=Bounds(0.0,1.0)` creates object each call. Could define `_BOUNDS = Bounds(0.0,1.0)` top-level. Use global to avoid repeated.
`_BOUNDS = Bounds(0.0,1.0)`.
Need `minimize` with `args` tuple; if function has fixed number args. Good.

Potential issue: `x0` for n changes; bounds scalar okay.
```

   return h,c

```
`localized_weights` function:
```
def localized_weights(n,c,K,sigma):
    full=2*n-1
    if K>full: K=full
    if K<=0: K=1
    # use argpartition for top K, but if K close full, use argsort
    if K < full//2:
       idx=np.argpartition(c, full-K)[full-K:]
    else:
       idx=np.argsort(c)[-K:]
    vals=c[idx]
    m=float(vals.max())
    w=np.exp((vals-m)/sigma)
    s=float(w.sum())
    if s <= 0:
       w=np.zeros_like(vals); w[int(np.argmax(vals))]=1.0; s=1.0
    else: w /= s
    W=np.zeros(full,float)
    W[idx]=w
    return W
```
If sigma small, exp okay. sigma as float positive. If vals-m underflows, only leader.

`choose top` for active sequence uses localized weights. Good.

`upsample`:
```
def upsample_value(h0,n0,n1):
    x0=(np.arange(n0)+0.5)*(2.0/n0)
    x1=(np.arange(n1)+0.5)*(2.0/n1)
    return np.interp(x1,x0,h0)
```
`hard_balanced`:
```
def hard_balanced(v,n):
    h=np.zeros(n,float)
    order=np.argsort(v)[::-1]
    h[order[:n//2]]=1.0
    return h
```
If ties order arbitrary. Good.
```

```
Let's implement helper phase/job deadline:
```
def allocate_deadline(frac, hard, min_s=20.0, reserve=10.0):
    now=time.time()
    rem = hard - max(reserve,0.0) - now
    if rem <= 0: return now
    dur = frac*rem
    dur = max(min_s, min(dur, rem*0.9))
    if dur > rem*0.5: dur = rem*0.5? No, can use more for last. Use min(rem*0.8).
    return min(hard-reserve, now+dur)

   rem=phase_end - time.time()
   if rem<3: return time.time()

def phase_deadline(frac, hard, min_s=10.0):
   now=time.time()
   usable = hard - 2.0 - now
   if usable < 4.0: return now
   dur = frac*usable
   if dur < min_s: dur = min(min_s, usable)
   if dur > usable: dur = usable
   return now+dur
```

`job_deadline(end,max_s)`:
```
def job_deadline(end,max_s,min_s=2.0):
   now=time.time(); rem=end-now
   if rem < min_s: return now
   dur=min(max_s, rem*0.9)
   if dur < min_s and rem >= min_s: dur=min_s
   return min(end-0.2, now+dur)
```

`fast_c_full`:
```
def fast_c_full(h,n,N):
    fa=np.fft.rfft(h,N)
    fb=np.fft.rfft(1.0-h,N)
    r=np.fft.irfft(fa*np.conj(fb),N)
    raw=np.empty(2*n-1,float)
    raw[:n-1]=r[-(n-1):]
    raw[n-1:]=r[:n]
    c=raw*(2.0/n)
    np.maximum(c,0.0,out=c)
    return float(c.max()), c

def phase_deadline(frac, hard, min_s=15.0):
   now=time.time()
   usable=hard-4.0-now
   if usable < 5.0: return now
   dur=frac*usable
   if dur < min_s: dur=min(min_s, usable)
   if dur > usable: dur=usable
   return now+dur
```

`project_box_sum`:
```
def project_box_sum(v, target):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=float)
    h = np.clip(v, 0.0, 1.0)
    s = float(h.sum())
    if abs(s - target) < 1e-10:
        h = finish_feasible(h, target)
        return h
    lo = float(np.min(v)) - 1.0
    hi = float(np.max(v)) + 1.0
    for _ in range(48):
        mid = 0.5*(lo+hi)
        s = float(np.clip(v-mid,0.0,1.0).sum())
        if s > target:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid
    t = 0.5*(lo+hi)
    h = np.clip(v-t,0.0,1.0)
    return finish_feasible(h, target)
```
If v contains nan, min nan. But v from finite. If minimize nan, guard before project? exact_update could get nan. Add at top:
```
if not np.all(np.isfinite(v)): v=np.zeros(v.shape)
```
Could be costly but okay.

`finish_feasible`:
```
def finish_feasible(h, target):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    for _ in range(3):
        res = float(target - h.sum())
        if abs(res) < 1e-13:
            break
        free = (h > 1e-12) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-12)
        if free.any():
            h[free] += res / float(free.sum())
        else:
            idx = int(np.argmax(1.0 - h)) if res > 0 else int(np.argmin(h))
            h[idx] += res
        h = np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
    # final one-point
    res = target - h.sum()
    if abs(res) > 1e-15:
        idx = int(np.argmax(1-h)) if res>0 else int(np.argmin(h))
        h[idx] += res
        h = np.clip(h,0,1)
    return h
```
If free distribution changes sum not exact due floating; loop. If free sum zero? handled. Final may clip if idx at bound and res large; but residual small.

Now, `exact_c5` if h sum not exactly? no.

`lbfgs_run`: Need pass `x0` copy? minimize may mutate? It likely copies. We can pass `x0` as is.
```
def lbfgs_run(fun, x0, args, maxiter, deadline):
    if deadline - time.time() < 2: return x0
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x0)): return x0
    global _TIMEOUT_DT, _LAST_XK
    _TIMEOUT_DT = max(time.time()+0.1, deadline - 0.5)
    _LAST_XK = None
    opts = {'maxiter': maxiter, 'ftol': 1e-14, 'gtol': 1e-8, 'maxls': 40}
    try:
       res = minimize(fun, x0, args=args, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, bounds=_BOUNDS, options=opts, callback=time_callback)
       xnew = res.x
    except OptTimeout:
       xnew = _LAST_XK if _LAST_XK is not None else x0
    except Exception:
       xnew = x0
    if xnew is None or not np.all(np.isfinite(xnew)):
       xnew = x0
    return xnew
```
If `res.status` invalid but x finite, okay. If `minimize` fails due tolerances, x. Good.
```

Implement `binary_raw_max(h,n,N)` using FFT to be faster for n up to 800:
```
def binary_raw_max(h,n,N):
    fa=np.fft.rfft(h,N)
    fb=np.fft.rfft(1.0-h,N)
    r=np.fft.irfft(fa*np.conj(fb),N)
    if n > 1:
       return float(max(r[-(n-1):].max(), r[:n].max()))
```
Because full max = max(negative part r[-(n-1):], lags0..n-1 r[:n]). Avoid concatenation. This returns max raw. Values ~counts. Use float. For n=400, 3 FFT 1024 (since need 799? N=1024) perhaps faster than direct. Good. Use this.

Let's define:
```
def raw_c_and_fa(h,n,N):
    fa=np.fft.rfft(h,N); fb=np.fft.rfft(1.0-h,N)
    r=np.fft.irfft(fa*np.conj(fb),N)
    raw=np.empty(2*n-1,float)
    if n>1: raw[:n-1]=r[-(n-1):]
    raw[n-1:]=r[:n]
    c=raw*(2.0/n); np.maximum(c,0.0,out=c)
    return c, fa
```
Then `fast_c_full` can be `c,fa=raw_c_and_fa(h,n,N); return float(c.max()),c`. `weighted_fun` calls `c,fa=raw_c_and_fa`. `smooth_fun` similarly. Good.

Can combine deadline helpers into compact:
```
def split_deadline(frac,hard,min_s=15):
    u=hard-3-time.time()
    return time.time() if u<5 else time.time()+min(u,max(min_s,frac*u))
def sub_deadline(end,max_s,min_s=2):
    r=end-time.time()
    return time.time() if r<3 else min(end-0.2,time.time()+min(max_s,0.9*r))
```

```
import numpy as np, time
from scipy.optimize import minimize, Bounds
class OptTimeout(Exception): pass
_BOUNDS=Bounds(0.0,1.0)
_TIMEOUT_DT=0.0
_LAST_XK=None
def time_callback(xk):
    global _LAST_XK
    if time.time()>_TIMEOUT_DT:
        _LAST_XK=np.array(xk,float).copy(); raise OptTimeout()
```

```
def raw_c_and_fa(h,n,N):
    fa=np.fft.rfft(h,N); fb=np.fft.rfft(1.0-h,N)
    r=np.fft.irfft(fa*np.conj(fb),N)
    raw=np.empty(2*n-1,float)
    if n>1: raw[:n-1]=r[-(n-1):]
    raw[n-1:]=r[:n]
    c=raw*(2.0/n)
    np.maximum(c,0.0,out=c)
    return c,fa
```

```
def weighted_grad(h,W,n,N,fa=None):
    G=np.zeros(n,float); w0=W[n-1]
    if w0: G+=w0*(1.0-2.0*h)
    if n==1: return G
    a=np.zeros(n); b=np.zeros(n)
    a[1:]=W[n-2::-1]; b[1:]=W[n:2*n-1]
    dz=np.abs(a[1:])+np.abs(b[1:])
    idxs=np.nonzero(dz>1e-14)[0]
    if len(idxs)<=48:
        for d in idxs:
            wm=a[d]; wp=b[d]; S=wm+wp
            if wm or S: G[:n-d]+=wm-S*h[d:]
            if wp or S: G[d:]+=wp-S*h[:n-d]
        return G
    if fa is None: fa=np.fft.rfft(h,N)
    s=a+b; ap=a[1:]; bp=b[1:]
    tail=np.concatenate((np.cumsum(ap[::-1])[::-1], np.array([0.0])))
    pref=np.concatenate((np.array([0.0]), np.cumsum(bp)))
    fs=np.fft.rfft(s,N)
    F=np.fft.irfft(fa*np.conj(fs),N)[:n]
    H=np.fft.irfft(fa*fs,N)[:n]
    G=w0*(1.0-2.0*h)+tail-F+pref-H
    return np.real(G)
```
If `wm` or `S` with float: `if wm != 0.0 or S !=0.0` but `wm or S` works for floats? bool(float) okay. If very small but nonzero > threshold? idx chosen a/b >1e-14, so okay.

```
def smooth_fun_lbg(x,n,alpha,N,pen):
    c,fa=raw_c_and_fa(x,n,N)
    m=float(c.max())
    z=alpha*(c-m)
    ez=np.exp(z)
    S=ez.sum(); w=ez/S if S>0 else (np.zeros_like(c) or ...)
```

`localized_weights`:
```
def localized_weights(n,c,K,sigma):
    full=2*n-1
    if K>full: K=full
    if K<1: K=1
    if K < full//2:
        idx=np.argpartition(c, full-K)[full-K:]
    else:
        idx=np.argsort(c)[-K:]
    v=c[idx]; m=float(v.max()); w=np.exp((v-m)/sigma); S=w.sum()
    if S <=0: w=np.zeros_like(v); w[int(np.argmax(v))]=1; S=1
    else: w=S? w /= S
    W=np.zeros(full,float); W[idx]=w
    return W
```
Potential `np.argpartition(c, full-K)` if full-K = full? K=0 but K>=1. If K=full, full-K=0 ok.

`hard_balanced`:
```
def hard_balanced(v,n):
    h=np.zeros(n,float)
    if n>1 and n%2==0:
       order=np.argsort(v)[::-1]
       h[order[:n//2]]=1.0
    else: h.fill(0.5)
    return h
```

`update_top_list`:
```
def update_top_list(top, cm, sh, k=8):
    if len(top)<k: top.append((cm,sh))
    else:
       mi=0
       for i in range(1,len(top)):
          if top[i][0] > top[mi][0]: mi=i
       if cm < top[mi][0]: top[mi]=(cm,sh)
```

```
def cyclic_refine(h,n,N,deadline,limit=400):
    h=np.asarray(h,float); best_c=exact_c5(h,n); best_h=h
    top=[]
    Cm,_ = raw_c_and_fa? Use fast_c_full but returns c. For shift0: cm,_=fast_c_full(h,n,N); top.append((cm,0))
    step=max(1, n//limit)
    for sh in range(0,n,step):
       if time.time()>deadline: break
       s=np.roll(h,sh)
       cm,_=fast_c_full(s,n,N); update_top_list(top,cm,sh)
    for csh,sh in top[:4]:
       for d in (-3,-2,-1,1,2,3):
          if time.time()>deadline: break
          sh2=(sh+d)%n; s=np.roll(h,sh2); cm,_=fast_c_full(s,n,N); update_top_list(top,cm,sh2)
    top.sort()
    seen=set(); 
    for cm,sh in top:
       if sh in seen: continue
       seen.add(sh)
       s=np.roll(h,sh); c=exact_c5(s,n)
       if c<best_c: best_c=c; best_h=s
    return best_h,best_c
```

`binary_raw_max`:
```
def binary_raw_max(h,n,N):
    fa=np.fft.rfft(h,N); fb=np.fft.rfft(1.0-h,N)
    r=np.fft.irfft(fa*np.conj(fb),N)
    if n>1:
       return float(max(r[-(n-1):].max(), r[:n].max()))
    return float(r[0])
```

`binary_sa`:
```
def binary_sa(h,n,N,deadline,maxit,rng):
    h=np.round(h).astype(float)
    target=n//2
    ones=np.where(h>0.5)[0]
    if len(ones)!=target:
       h=hard_balanced(h,rng.rand? no) # need balanced. Use h plus random? We can sort current h: order=np.argsort(h)[::-1]; h=np.zeros(n); h[order[:target]]=1
    ones=np.where(h>0.5)[0].tolist(); zeros=np.where(h<=0.5)[0].tolist()
    cur=float(binary_raw_max(h,n,N)); best=h.copy(); bestm=cur; temp=3.0
    decay=0.9995
    for _ in range(maxit):
       if time.time()>deadline: break
       zi=rng.randint(len(zeros)); oi=rng.randint(len(ones)); i=zeros[zi]; j=ones[oi]
       h[i]=1.0; h[j]=0.0
       new=float(binary_raw_max(h,n,N)); delta=new-cur
       if delta < 1e-9 or (delta>0 and temp>0.05 and rng.rand() < np.exp(-delta/temp)):
          cur=new; zeros[zi]=j; ones[oi]=i
          if cur < bestm: bestm=cur; best=h.copy()
       else:
          h[i]=0.0; h[j]=1.0
       temp*=decay
       if temp<0.05: temp=0.05
    return best,bestm*(2.0/n)
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize, Bounds


class OptTimeout(Exception):
    pass


_BOUNDS = Bounds(0.0, 1.0)
_TIMEOUT_DT = 0.0
_LAST_XK = None


def time_callback(xk):
    global _LAST_XK
    if time.time() > _TIMEOUT_DT:
        _LAST_XK = np.array(xk, dtype=float).copy()
        raise OptTimeout()


def fft_len_for(n):
    need = 2 * n - 1
    m = 1
    while m < need:
        m <<= 1
    return m


def finish_feasible(h, target):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    for _ in range(3):
        res = float(target - h.sum())
        if abs(res) < 1e-13:
            break
        free = (h > 1e-12) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-12)
        if free.any():
            h[free] += res / float(free.sum())
        else:
            idx = int(np.argmax(1.0 - h)) if res > 0 else int(np.argmin(h))
            h[idx] += res
        h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    res = float(target - h.sum())
    if abs(res) > 1e-15:
        idx = int(np.argmax(1.0 - h)) if res > 0 else int(np.argmin(h))
        h[idx] += res
        h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    return h


def project_box_sum(v, target):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=float)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(v)):
        v = np.zeros_like(v, dtype=float)
    h = np.clip(v, 0.0, 1.0)
    if abs(float(h.sum()) - target) < 1e-10:
        return finish_feasible(h, target)
    lo = float(np.min(v)) - 1.0
    hi = float(np.max(v)) + 1.0
    for _ in range(48):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        s = float(np.clip(v - mid, 0.0, 1.0).sum())
        if s > target:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid
    t = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
    h = np.clip(v - t, 0.0, 1.0)
    return finish_feasible(h, target)


def exact_c5(h, n):
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full") * (2.0 / n)
    c = float(np.max(c))
    return c if np.isfinite(c) else 1e100


def exact_update(h, n):
    h = project_box_sum(h, 0.5 * n)
    return h, exact_c5(h, n)


def raw_c_and_fa(h, n, N):
    fa = np.fft.rfft(h, N)
    fb = np.fft.rfft(1.0 - h, N)
    r = np.fft.irfft(fa * np.conj(fb), N)
    raw = np.empty(2 * n - 1, dtype=float)
    if n > 1:
        raw[:n - 1] = r[-(n - 1):]
    raw[n - 1:] = r[:n]
    c = raw * (2.0 / n)
    np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)
    return c, fa


def fast_c_full(h, n, N):
    c, _ = raw_c_and_fa(h, n, N)
    return float(np.max(c)), c


def weighted_grad(h, W, n, N, fa=None):
    G = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    w0 = W[n - 1]
    if w0:
        G += w0 * (1.0 - 2.0 * h)
    if n == 1:
        return G
    a = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    b = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    a[1:] = W[n - 2::-1]
    b[1:] = W[n:2 * n - 1]
    dz = np.abs(a[1:]) + np.abs(b[1:])
    idxs = np.nonzero(dz > 1e-14)[0]
    if len(idxs) <= 48:
        for d in idxs:
            wm = a[d]
            wp = b[d]
            S = wm + wp
            if wm or S:
                G[:n - d] += wm - S * h[d:]
            if wp or S:
                G[d:] += wp - S * h[:n - d]
        return G
    if fa is None:
        fa = np.fft.rfft(h, N)
    s = a + b
    ap = a[1:]
    bp = b[1:]
    tail = np.concatenate((np.cumsum(ap[::-1])[::-1], np.array([0.0])))
    pref = np.concatenate((np.array([0.0]), np.cumsum(bp)))
    fs = np.fft.rfft(s, N)
    F = np.fft.irfft(fa * np.conj(fs), N)[:n]
    H = np.fft.irfft(fa * fs, N)[:n]
    G = w0 * (1.0 - 2.0 * h) + tail - F + pref - H
    return np.real(G)


def smooth_fun_lbg(x, n, alpha, N, pen):
    dx = 2.0 / n
    c, fa = raw_c_and_fa(x, n, N)
    m = float(np.max(c))
    ez = np.exp(alpha * (c - m))
    S = float(ez.sum())
    if S <= 0.0:
        idx = int(np.argmax(c))
        w = np.zeros_like(c)
        w[idx] = 1.0
        S = 1.0
    else:
        w = ez / S
    f = m + np.log(S) / alpha
    g = weighted_grad(x, dx * w, n, N, fa)
    s = float(x.sum() - 0.5 * n)
    if pen:
        f += 0.5 * pen * s * s
        g = g + pen * s
    return f, g


def weighted_fun(x, n, N, Wnorm, pen):
    dx = 2.0 / n
    c, fa = raw_c_and_fa(x, n, N)
    f = float(np.dot(c, Wnorm))
    g = weighted_grad(x, dx * Wnorm, n, N, fa)
    s = float(x.sum() - 0.5 * n)
    if pen:
        f += 0.5 * pen * s * s
        g = g + pen * s
    return f, g


def lbfgs_run(fun, x0, args, maxiter, deadline):
    if deadline - time.time() < 2.0:
        return x0
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x0)):
        return x0
    global _TIMEOUT_DT, _LAST_XK
    _TIMEOUT_DT = max(time.time() + 0.1, deadline - 0.5)
    _LAST_XK = None
    opts = {"maxiter": maxiter, "ftol": 1e-14, "gtol": 1e-8, "maxls": 40}
    try:
        res = minimize(fun, x0, args=args, method="L-BFGS-B", jac=True,
                       bounds=_BOUNDS, options=opts, callback=time_callback)
        xnew = res.x
    except OptTimeout:
        xnew = _LAST_XK if _LAST_XK is not None else x0
    except Exception:
        xnew = x0
    if xnew is None or not np.all(np.isfinite(xnew)):
        xnew = x0
    return xnew


def split_deadline(frac, hard, min_s=15.0):
    u = hard - 3.0 - time.time()
    if u < 5.0:
        return time.time()
    return time.time() + min(u, max(min_s, frac * u))


def sub_deadline(end, max_s, min_s=2.0):
    r = end - time.time()
    if r < min_s:
        return time.time()
    return min(end - 0.2, time.time() + max(min_s, min(max_s, 0.9 * r)))


def localized_weights(n, c, K, sigma):
    full = 2 * n - 1
    if K > full:
        K = full
    if K < 1:
        K = 1
    if K < full // 2:
        idx = np.argpartition(c, full - K)[full - K:]
    else:
        idx = np.argsort(c)[-K:]
    v = c[idx]
    v = v - float(v.max())
    w = np.exp(v / sigma)
    S = float(w.sum())
    if S <= 0.0:
        w = np.zeros_like(w)
        w[int(np.argmax(v))] = 1.0
        S = 1.0
    else:
        w /= S
    W = np.zeros(full, dtype=float)
    W[idx] = w
    return W


def upsample_value(h0, n0, n1):
    x0 = (np.arange(n0) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n0)
    x1 = (np.arange(n1) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n1)
    return np.interp(x1, x0, h0)


def hard_balanced(v, n):
    h = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    if n > 1 and n % 2 == 0:
        order = np.argsort(v)[::-1]
        h[order[:n // 2]] = 1.0
    else:
        h.fill(0.5)
    return h


def update_top_list(top, cm, sh, k=8):
    if len(top) < k:
        top.append((cm, sh))
    else:
        mi = 0
        for i in range(1, len(top)):
            if top[i][0] > top[mi][0]:
                mi = i
        if cm < top[mi][0]:
            top[mi] = (cm, sh)


def cyclic_refine(h, n, N, deadline, limit=400):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    best_h = h
    best_c = exact_c5(h, n)
    top = []
    cm, _ = fast_c_full(h, n, N)
    update_top_list(top, cm, 0)
    step = max(1, n // limit)
    for sh in range(0, n, step):
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break
        s = np.roll(h, sh)
        cm, _ = fast_c_full(s, n, N)
        update_top_list(top, cm, sh)
    for _c, sh in top[:4]:
        for d in (-3, -2, -1, 1, 2, 3):
            if time.time() > deadline:
                break
            sh2 = (sh + d) % n
            s = np.roll(h, sh2)
            cm, _ = fast_c_full(s, n, N)
            update_top_list(top, cm, sh2)
    top.sort()
    seen = set()
    for _c, sh in top:
        if sh in seen:
            continue
        seen.add(sh)
        s = np.roll(h, sh)
        c = exact_c5(s, n)
        if c < best_c:
            best_c = c
            best_h = s
    return best_h, best_c


def binary_raw_max(h, n, N):
    fa = np.fft.rfft(h, N)
    fb = np.fft.rfft(1.0 - h, N)
    r = np.fft.irfft(fa * np.conj(fb), N)
    if n > 1:
        return float(max(float(r[-(n - 1):].max()), float(r[:n].max())))
    return float(r[0])


def binary_sa(h, n, N, deadline, maxit, rng):
    h = np.round(np.asarray(h, dtype=float))
    target = n // 2
    ones = np.where(h > 0.5)[0]
    if len(ones) != target:
        h = hard_balanced(h, n)
    ones = np.where(h > 0.5)[0].tolist()
    zeros = np.where(h <= 0.5)[0].tolist()
    cur = float(binary_raw_max(h, n, N))
    best = h.copy()
    bestm = cur
    temp = 3.0
    decay = 0.9995
    for _ in range(maxit):
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break
        zi = rng.randint(len(zeros))
        oi = rng.randint(len(ones))
        i = zeros[zi]
        j = ones[oi]
        h[i] = 1.0
        h[j] = 0.0
        new = float(binary_raw_max(h, n, N))
        delta = new - cur
        if delta < 1e-9 or (delta > 0.0 and temp > 0.05 and rng.rand() < np.exp(-delta / temp)):
            cur = new
            zeros[zi] = j
            ones[oi] = i
            if cur < bestm:
                bestm = cur
                best = h.copy()
        else:
            h[i] = 0.0
            h[j] = 1.0
        temp = max(0.05, temp * decay)
    return best, bestm * (2.0 / n)


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    hard = t0 + max(60.0, float(budget_s) - 12.0)
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)

    try:
        h0 = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
    except Exception:
        h0 = None

    if h0 is None or len(h0) == 0:
        n = 400
        h0 = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
        order = np.argsort(rng.rand(n))
        h0[order[:n // 2]] = 1.0

    n = int(len(h0))
    if n % 2:
        h0 = h0[:-1]
        n -= 1
    N = fft_len_for(n)

    best_h = project_box_sum(h0, 0.5 * n)
    best_c = exact_c5(best_h, n)

    if time.time() < hard - 20:
        best_h, best_c = cyclic_refine(best_h, n, N, hard - 10, 400)

    if n <= 600 and best_c > 0.3800 and time.time() < hard - 90:
        hbin = hard_balanced(best_h + 0.05 * rng.normal(size=n), n)
        hl, cl = binary_sa(hbin, n, N, min(hard - 70, time.time() + 90), 100000, rng)
        if cl < best_c:
            best_h, best_c = exact_update(hl, n)
        if time.time() < hard - 40:
            best_h, best_c = cyclic_refine(best_h, n, N, hard - 20, 200)

    pe = split_deadline(0.22, hard, 20)
    alpha = 50.0
    for _ in range(6):
        if time.time() > pe - 3:
            break
        maxit = 400 if n <= 500 else 250
        je = sub_deadline(pe, 70)
        x = lbfgs_run(smooth_fun_lbg, best_h, (n, alpha, N, 100000.0), maxit, je)
        h2, c2 = exact_update(x, n)
        if c2 < best_c:
            best_h, best_c = h2, c2
        alpha *= 6.0

    pe = split_deadline(0.32, hard, 20)
    for Ki in range(5):
        K = 1 << (Ki + 2)
        sigma = 1e-4
        for _ in range(4):
            if time.time() > pe - 3:
                break
            _, cv = fast_c_full(best_h, n, N)
            W = localized_weights(n, cv, K, sigma)
            maxit = 500 if n <= 500 else 300
            je = sub_deadline(pe, 50)
            x = lbfgs_run(weighted_fun, best_h, (n, N, W, 100000.0), maxit, je)
            h2, c2 = exact_update(x, n)
            if c2 < best_c:
                best_h, best_c = h2, c2
            sigma *= 3.0
    if time.time() < hard - 30:
        best_h, best_c = cyclic_refine(best_h, n, N, hard - 20, 300)

    pe = split_deadline(0.20, hard, 20)
    scale = 0.001
    for si in range(8):
        if time.time() > pe - 3:
            break
        if si < 2:
            hstart = np.roll(best_h, int(rng.randint(n)))
        else:
            hstart = best_h + scale * rng.normal(size=n)
        hstart = project_box_sum(hstart, 0.5 * n)
        _, cv = fast_c_full(hstart, n, N)
        W = localized_weights(n, cv, 16, 3e-4)
        maxit = 300 if n <= 500 else 200
        je = sub_deadline(pe, 30)
        x = lbfgs_run(weighted_fun, hstart, (n, N, W, 100000.0), maxit, je)
        h2, c2 = exact_update(x, n)
        if c2 < best_c:
            best_h, best_c = h2, c2
        if time.time() < pe - 8:
            je = sub_deadline(pe, 20)
            x = lbfgs_run(smooth_fun_lbg, best_h, (n, 50000.0, N, 100000.0), maxit, je)
            h2, c2 = exact_update(x, n)
            if c2 < best_c:
                best_h, best_c = h2, c2
        scale *= 2.0

    pe = split_deadline(0.26, hard, 30)
    for cx in (600, 800, 1000):
        if time.time() > pe - 40:
            break
        if cx <= n:
            continue
        if cx > 1000 and time.time() > hard - 120:
            break
        hup = upsample_value(best_h, n, cx)
        h1 = project_box_sum(hup, 0.5 * cx)
        h2 = project_box_sum(hard_balanced(hup, cx), 0.5 * cx)
        c1 = exact_c5(h1, cx)
        c2 = exact_c5(h2, cx)
        hwork = h1 if c1 <= c2 else h2
        cwork = c1 if c1 <= c2 else c2
        if cwork < best_c:
            best_h, best_c, n = hwork, cwork, cx
            N = fft_len_for(n)
        if cwork > best_c + 1e-3:
            continue
        Nc = fft_len_for(cx) if n != cx else N
        hcur = best_h if n == cx else hwork
        if time.time() < pe - 20:
            for qi in range(2):
                if time.time() > pe - 15:
                    break
                ca = 2000.0 * (10 ** qi)
                je = sub_deadline(pe, 80)
                x = lbfgs_run(smooth_fun_lbg, hcur, (cx, ca, Nc, 100000.0), 200, je)
                hnew, cnew = exact_update(x, cx)
                if cnew < best_c:
                    best_h, best_c, n = hnew, cnew, cx
                    N = fft_len_for(n)
                if cnew < cwork or n == cx:
                    hcur, cwork = hnew, cnew
            for Ki in range(3):
                K = 8 << Ki
                sigma = 1e-4
                for sj in range(2):
                    if time.time() > pe - 20:
                        break
                    if n == cx:
                        Nc = N
                    _, cv = fast_c_full(hcur, cx, Nc)
                    W = localized_weights(cx, cv, K, sigma)
                    je = sub_deadline(pe, 70)
                    x = lbfgs_run(weighted_fun, hcur, (cx, Nc, W, 100000.0), 200, je)
                    hnew, cnew = exact_update(x, cx)
                    if cnew < best_c:
                        best_h, best_c, n = hnew, cnew, cx
                        N = fft_len_for(n)
                    if cnew < cwork or n == cx:
                        hcur, cwork = hnew, cnew
                    sigma *= 3.0
        if n == cx and time.time() < pe - 15:
            hcy, ccy = cyclic_refine(hcur, cx, Nc, min(hard - 20, pe), 200)
            if ccy < best_c:
                best_h, best_c, n = hcy, ccy, cx
                N = fft_len_for(n)

    if time.time() < hard - 15:
        for ca in (100000.0, 500000.0, 2000000.0):
            if time.time() > hard - 8:
                break
            je = min(hard - 5, time.time() + 30)
            x = lbfgs_run(smooth_fun_lbg, best_h, (n, ca, N, 100000.0), 150, je)
            h2, c2 = exact_update(x, n)
            if c2 < best_c:
                best_h, best_c = h2, c2

    if time.time() < hard - 10:
        _, cv = fast_c_full(best_h, n, N)
        for Ki in range(2):
            K = 8 * (2 ** Ki)
            sigma = 2e-5 * (3 ** Ki)
            W = localized_weights(n, cv, K, sigma)
            je = min(hard - 5, time.time() + 20)
            x = lbfgs_run(weighted_fun, best_h, (n, N, W, 100000.0), 150, je)
            h2, c2 = exact_update(x, n)
            if c2 < best_c:
                best_h, best_c = h2, c2

    if time.time() < hard - 5:
        best_h, best_c = cyclic_refine(best_h, n, N, hard - 2, 300)

    best_h = project_box_sum(best_h, 0.5 * n)
    best_c = exact_c5(best_h, n)
    return best_h, best_c, int(n)
```
